Potential robust approach: Use a heuristic called "convolution flattening via multiplicative updates" (like entropy maximization). Since objective wants minimize max of b_k = ∑ p_i p_{k-i}. This is analogous to finding a probability vector whose "sum distribution" is as uniform as possible. We can iterate: Given current sum distribution b, we want increase p_i where it contributes less to peaked lags? Actually to reduce peaks, decrease p_i that multiply with many p_j causing peaks; increase p_i contributing to low lags. Could use gradient descent on log p.

Define score coefficient h = max b (with sum1). Subgradient direction to reduce h for active peak k: decrease p_i proportional to p_{k-i}. Multiplicative update p_i ← p_i * exp(-η g_i) where g_i = (p_{k-i}+p_{i-k}) for active peaks. Nonnegative. Then renormalize. This is exactly exponentiated gradient for the nonconvex bilinear? It might move mass away from symmetric pairs. Starting uniform, active k central gradient constant, no move. Need break symmetry: add small random or structured initial perturbation. Then gradient descent may find asymmetric minima. We can use many random restarts with different asymmetries (e.g. geometric, sinusoidal) and optimize.

Let's outline multiplicative gradient for p∈R^n sum1:
- Compute b = p*p via fftconvolve or np.convolve (for n moderate). Determine active set where b≥h-eps. For each active k, subgradient g_k(i)=p_{k-i}+p_{i-k} (if in range). Average or sum. Choose g_i. Update p_i *= exp(-α g_i) (or p_i += -eta g_i with clipping). Renormalize. Since g_i can be large, use step based on h.
- This is like projected multiplicative weights for minimizing f? For nonconvex may converge.

Implement objective/gradient:
Given z shape n:
  zm = z - max(z)
  ex = exp(zm)
  p = ex / sum(ex)
  b = fft_convsquare(p, n)
  score = 2n * b
  peak = score.max()
  weights = active weights
  H = weighted sum of shifted p
  grad_p = 4n H
  mean = dot(p, grad_p)
  grad_z = p * (grad_p - mean)
  value = softmax_value (or peak + maybe)

1. `fft_square(p, n)`:
```
N = 1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
fr = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
b = np.fft.irfft(fr*fr, N)[:2*n-1]
# clean negative tiny
if b.min() < 0 and b.min() > -1e-12: b = np.maximum(b,0)
return b
```
For p sum1, b nonnegative. FFT error could produce tiny negative; max unaffected if negative tiny. For gradient, weights based on b; negative fine. Could use `np.convolve` for validation.

2. `active_gradient(p, b, tau, max_active, select_delta)`:

   # compute top16 by argpartition

```

3. Piecewise z objective:
```
def _z_to_p(z, block, D, n):
   zm = z - z.max()
   e = np.exp(zm)
   pbin = e/e.sum()
   p = np.repeat(pbin, block)
   if n != len(p): p=p[:n] (should exact)
   return p

def _z_score_grad(z, block, D, n, tau, max_active):
   p=_z_to_p(...)
   b=fft_square(p,n)
   val,g=active_gradient(p,b,tau,max_active)

   gb = g.reshape(D, block).sum(axis=1)  # if block exact
   grad = block * pbin * (gb - sumpg*block)
   # maybe scale? grad wrt z.
   return val, grad

def mirror_optimize(p, n, time_budget, tau, max_active, eta0=0.3):
  p=copy normalized
  best=p.copy(); best_score=score_fft(p)
  eta=eta0
  stall=0
  while time.monotonic()<deadline:
    b=fft_square(p,n); val,g,_=active_gradient(p,b,tau,max_active)
    # exact score maybe val approx; compute peak = 2n*b.max()
    peak = 2n*b.max()
    if peak < best_score - 1e-9: update best, stall=0 else stall++
    # gradient in score units g (4nH)
    maxg = g.max()
    if maxg <= 1e-12: break
    logp = np.log(p + 1e-300)
    # Center by maxg to keep exp moderate: logp += eta*(maxg - g)
    # Maybe subtract weighted mean of (maxg-g)? constant irrelevant.
    factor = np.exp(-eta*(g - maxg))  # since maxg-g >=0, factor <=1? Actually if g lower, factor larger if g<maxg.
    p_new = p*factor
    p_new = np.maximum(p_new,0); s=p_new.sum(); if s<=0 break; p_new/=s
    # evaluate p_new exact maybe score_fft; if improves accept, else try smaller eta
    peak_new=2*n*fft_square(p_new,n).max()
    if peak_new < peak:
      p=p_new; best...
      if stall>5: eta*=0.8? maybe increase? 
    else:
      eta *= 0.7; if eta<1e-4 break? 
```

```

def _z_fun(z, D, block, n, tau, max_active, select_delta):
   q=softmax(z)
   p=np.repeat(q/block, block)
   b=fft_square(p,n)
   val, g, _=active_gradient(p,b,tau,max_active,select_delta)
   return val

def _z_grad(z,...):
   q=softmax(z); p=...
   b=...; val,g,_=active_gradient...
   Gmean = g.reshape(D,block).sum(axis=1)/block
   S = np.dot(p,g)
   return q*(Gmean - S)
```

def mirror_optimize(p, n, tau, max_active, select_delta, max_seconds, seed=None):
 deadline = time.monotonic()+max_seconds
 p = p.copy(); p /= p.sum()
 best=p.copy(); best_score = fft_score(p) # maybe direct later
 eta = 0.4
 bad = 0
 while time.monotonic() < deadline:
   b=fft_square(p,n)
   val,g,_=active_gradient(p,b,tau,max_active,select_delta)
   peak=2n*b.max()
   if peak < best_score - 1e-10:
      best=p.copy(); best_score=peak; eta = min(1.0, eta*1.1)
   else:
      bad +=1
      eta *= 0.7
      if bad>20 and eta<0.01: break
   maxg=g.max()
   if maxg <= 1e-14: break
   # center by weighted mean? log update exp(-eta*(g - mean_g)) maybe stable
   mean_g = np.mean(g) # or dot? 
   # To keep exp bounded, use maxg:
   factor = np.exp(-eta * (g - maxg)) # if g small, factor exp(eta*(maxg-g)) <= exp(eta*maxg)
   # if maxg large, exp overflow? eta*maxg maybe >700? g scale 4nH; H max maybe 1? g max 16384? eta .4 =>6554 exp overflow. Need scale.

Full gradient H via FFT code:
```
def weighted_shift_sum_fft(p, w):
    n=len(p)
    if n==1: return np.array([p[0]*w[0]])
    N=1 << ((3*n-2).bit_length())
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], N)
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(fp*fw, N)
    H=conv[2*n-2 - np.arange(n)]
    # due FFT error tiny imaginary? irfft real.
    return H
```
But w length 2n-1, w[::-1] length. Good.
Can we exploit symmetry to use real? fine.

Pseudo:
```
def mirror_optimize(p, n, max_seconds, tau, max_active, select_delta):
    deadline = time.monotonic()+max_seconds
    p=np.array(p,float); p=np.clip(p,0,None); s=p.sum(); if s<=0: p=np.ones(n)/n; else p/=s
    best=p.copy()
    best_score=fft_score_array(p)
    eta=0.2
    stall=0
    iter=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       b=fft_square(p,n); score=2n*b; val,g=active_gradient(p,b,tau,max_active,select_delta)
       peak=float(score.max())
       iter+=1
       # try line search
       accepted=False
       for trial in [eta, 0.5*eta, 0.25*eta]:
          if trial <=1e-5: break
          center = float(np.mean(g))
          exp_arg = -trial*(g-center)
          np.clip(exp_arg, -50, 50, out=exp_arg)
          p_new = p * np.exp(exp_arg)
          p_new = np.where(np.isfinite(p_new), p_new, 0)
          s=np.sum(p_new)
          if s<=0: continue
          p_new /= s
          new_score=fft_score_array(p_new)
          if new_score < peak - 1e-12:
             p=p_new; peak=new_score; accepted=True
             if new_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best=p.copy(); best_score=new_score
             eta=min(1.0, eta*1.1)
             stall=0
             break
       if not accepted:
          stall +=1
          eta *= 0.6
          if eta < 1e-4:
             # small random nudge then reset eta?
             if stall > 30: break
       # optional if no improvement for 50, reduce tau? But tau fixed.
    return best
```
Potential bug: peak used from current b; new_score < peak (current exact peak). Good. But `fft_score_array` compute b again; if new_score lower than current peak, accept. Doesn't matter after normalization but changes relative scaling? Center constant cancels. Any constant; choose `np.median(g)` to reduce range. We'll use unweighted mean for simplicity. Let's implement helper `_q_to_p(q, block, n)`:
```
def _q_to_p(q, block, n):
   e=np.exp(q - q.max())
   e = np.where(np.isfinite(e), e, 0)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: q=np.zeros(len(q)); e=np.ones(len(q)); s=e.sum()
   else: e/=s
   p=np.repeat(e/block, block)
   if p.size != n: p = p[:n]
   s=p.sum()
   if s<=0: p=np.ones(n)/n
   else: p/=s
   return p
```
Since n=D*block.

Implementation of `_score_gradient_full(p,b,tau)`:
```
def _score_gradient_full(p,b,tau):
    n=len(p)
    s=2.0*n*b
    # sym s
    s = 0.5*(s + s[::-1])
    peak = float(s.max())
    shift = s - peak
    ez = np.exp(shift/tau)
    sumW = float(ez.sum())
    if sumW <=0 or not finite:
       # fallback single max
       idx=int(np.argmax(s)); w=np.zeros(2*n-1); w[idx]=1; val=peak; g=... active single? 
    else:
       w=ez/sumW
    H=weighted_shift_sum_fft(p,w)
    g=4.0*n*H
    val=peak + tau*np.log(sumW)
    return float(val), g
```
If tau very small and only one peak, w one-hot, H via FFT of sparse; could use active for efficiency, but full okay. But if sumW=1 due underflow all others 0, val=peak. H via FFT still correct. Good.

Now, direct/fft score functions should normalize? For p from softmax sum1. For fallback, normalize. Use:
```
def fft_score_array(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0); p=np.clip(p,0,None)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0: return inf
    p=p/s
    n=p.size
    b=fft_square(p,n)
    return float(2*n*np.max(b))
```
This normalizes, good.

`direct_score_array`:
```
def direct_score_array(p):
   p=np.asarray(p,float).copy()
   p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0); p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
   s=p.sum()
   if s<0.01: return inf
   b=np.convolve(p,p)
   if b.size==0 or not finite: inf
   return float(2*len(p)*np.max(b)/(s*s))
```
Evaluator clips negative anyway; direct matches.

`fft_square` should take normalized p. Implement with N:
```
def fft_square(p,n):
   N=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
   fr=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
   b=np.fft.irfft(fr*fr,N)[:2*n-1]
   if b.size:
       # if negative significant due num, clip small negative
       mn=min(0.0, b.min())
       if mn < 0 and mn > -1e-12*max(1.0,np.max(b)): b=np.maximum(b,0)
       else b=np.maximum(b,0) # for nonnegative true, clip all negatives? FFT error could be -1e-15; but if large negative not.
   return b
```
For true convolution nonnegative; clipping all negative to 0 safe. It slightly changes score if negative due poor FFT? not.

Final zeroing:
```
def try_zeroize(p, factor=1e-7):
   if p.max()<=0: return p
   p2=p.copy()
   p2[p2 < p.max()*factor]=0
   s=p2.sum(); if s<=0: return p
   p2/=s
   sc=direct_score_array(p2)
   if finite and sc<direct_score_array(p): return p2
   return p
```

```
def _z_grad(z, D, block, n, tau):
   p=_z_to_p(z,D,block,n) # needs D? q len D
   q=_z_to_q(z,D) maybe recompute. To avoid recompute, in _z_to_p we could return p,q? But helper can compute. We'll create `_softmax_z(z)` returns q.
   b=fft_square(p,n)
   s=2*n*b; s=0.5*(s+s[::-1])
   peak=s.max(); ez=np.exp((s-peak)/tau); sumW=ez.sum()
   if sumW<=0: idx=int(np.argmax(s)); w=np.zeros(2*n-1); w[idx]=1; sumW=1
   else: w=ez/sumW
   H=weighted_shift_sum_fft(p,w)
   g=4n*H
   Gmean = g.reshape(D,block).sum(axis=1)/block
   S = dot(p,g)
   grad = q*(Gmean - S)
   if not finite: return zeros
   return grad
```
`_softmax_z` stable:
```
def _softmax_z(z):
   z=np.asarray(z,float)
   zm=z-z.max()
   e=np.exp(zm)
   e=np.where(np.isfinite(e),e,0)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: e=np.ones_like(z); s=e.sum()
   else: e=e/s
   return e
```

`_z_to_p` can call `_softmax_z` and repeat. This recomputes in fun and grad; okay. Could have `_z_to_p_no_q` but fine.

Now, active_gradient for mirror with full? It should use `b` from fft. For p length n, if b has slight asym, sym? For active, no need full sym, but use sym. Implementation:
```
def active_gradient(p,b,tau,max_active,select_delta):
   n=len(p); s=2*n*b; s=0.5*(s+s[::-1]); peak=s.max()
   delta=max(select_delta, 3*tau)
   mask=s >= peak - delta
   if not mask.any(): active=np.array([int(np.argmax(s))])
   else:
       active=np.where(mask)[0]
       if len(active)>max_active:
           active=np.argpartition(s, -max_active)[-max_active:]
   ez=np.exp((s[active]-peak)/tau); sumW=ez.sum()
   if sumW<=0: active=np.array([int(np.argmax(s))]); ez=np.array([1.0]); sumW=1.0
   w=ez/sumW
   H=np.zeros(n,float)
   for kk,ww in zip(active,w):
       k=int(kk)
       if ww<=0: continue
       if k < n:
           H[:k+1] += ww * p[k::-1]
       else:
           t=k-n+1
           H[t:] += ww * p[n-1:t-2:-1]
   g=4*n*H
   val=peak + tau*np.log(sumW)
   return val,g
```
If active top via argpartition unsorted; order irrelevant.

Potential overflow in exp if tau tiny and s[active]-peak=0 for max and negative up to -delta. If delta=3tau, min exp e^-3 ~0.05; no overflow. Good.

```
def optimize_piecewise(n,D,block,z_starts,deadline,best_p,best_score,max_starts=6,stage_defs=None):
    if stage_defs is None: stage_defs=...
    nstarts=min(len(z_starts),max_starts)
    for si in range(nstarts):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 180: break
       z=z_starts[si].astype(float).copy()
       for idx,(tau,maxiter) in enumerate(stage_defs):
          if time.monotonic() > deadline - 150: break
          maxit=max(20,int(maxiter))
          try: res=minimize(...)
          except Exception: res=None
          if res is not None and finite: z=res.x
          p=_z_to_p(z,D,block,n)
          ff=fft_score_array(p)
          if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60:
             ex=direct_score_array(p)
             if ex<best_score: best_score=ex; best_p=p.copy()
          print(f"n={n} ...")
    return best_p,best_score
```

`zeroize_candidate` only modifies, no score:
```
def zeroize_candidate(p, factor=1e-7):
   p=np.asarray(p,float).copy()
   if p.size==0: return p
   mx=p.max()
   if mx<=0: return p
   p[p < mx*factor] = 0
   s=p.sum()
   if s<=0: return p
   return p/s
```

One more thought: In `_z_grad`, the gradient wrt z of soft max value: `grad = q * (Gmean - S)`. We derived. But if using `_z_fun` value with full weights but `_z_grad` with same full weights. The derivative wrt q (bin probabilities) of F is q*(Gmean - S)? Let's rederive with q total mass, p=q/block. F gradient wrt p is g. F as function of q: F(q)=F(p_i=q_s/block). dF/dq_s =∑_i in s g_i * (1/block) = (1/block) G_s. Then softmax constrained gradient: over q simplex, gradient for z_s = q_s[(dF/dq_s) - ∑ q_t dF/dq_t]. dF/dq_s = G_s/block. T=∑q_t G_t/block = S. So grad_z_s = q_s(G_s/block - S). Yes. Good.

If block=1, dF/dq_s = g_s, T=dot(p,g)=S, grad=p(g-S). correct.

Potential issue: Since `_q_to_p` normalizes p, q sum1 but p normalization could slightly change if repeat sum not exactly 1; gradient formula assumes exact p=sum q/block. Normalization due rounding negligible. Could omit normalization in `_q_to_p` for gradient consistency? But p sum close; normalization can introduce tiny inconsistency. Use q normalized and p=sum exactly? `p=np.repeat(q / block, block); p /= p.sum()`. If p.sum differs by 1e-16, gradient slight. fine. Could skip normalization and rely on q sum1; but due float sum maybe exact enough. Let's keep normalization.

Now, adjust `make_z_starts` to ensure z not constant: if `np.allclose(z, z[0])`, add tiny random. Not necessary.

Let's craft final code. Need be within final only code block.
</think>

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step-function coefficients minimizing
2*n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2.

Method:
- Work with sum-normalized probabilities.
- Optimize piecewise-constant probability vectors by L-BFGS on log-bin masses.
- Use FFT convolutions for fast evaluation and a smooth soft-max of the
  convolution coefficients as the objective.
- Polish the full-length vector with softmax/full L-BFGS and then with a
  multiplicative active-peak descent routine.
- Keep the best exact sequence found under a time budget.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    _HAS_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAS_SCIPY = False


def _softmax_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    zm = z - np.max(z)
    e = np.exp(zm)
    e = np.where(np.isfinite(e), e, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        e = np.ones(z.shape, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(e.size)
    else:
        e /= s
    return e


def _q_to_p(q, block, n):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.repeat(q / float(max(1, int(block))), int(block))
    if p.size != n:
        p = p[:n]
    if p.size == 0:
        return p
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        p /= s
    return p


def _z_to_p(z, D, block, n):
    q = _softmax_z(z)
    return _q_to_p(q, block, n)


def fft_square(p, n):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(n)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    N = 1 << max(1, need.bit_length())
    fr = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fr * fr, N)[:need]
    if b.size:
        b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return b


def fft_score_array(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    p = p / s
    n = p.size
    b = fft_square(p, n)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(b))


def direct_score_array(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    if p.size == 1:
        return 2.0
    b = np.convolve(p, p)
    mx = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * p.size * mx / (s * s))


def weighted_shift_sum_fft(p, w):
    """Compute H_i = sum_j p_j w_{i+j}, for i=0,...,n-1."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if w.size != 2 * n - 1:
        tmp = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(tmp.size, w.size)
        tmp[:m] = w[:m]
        w = tmp
    w_rev = w[::-1]
    need = n + w.size - 1
    N = 1 << max(1, need.bit_length())
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w_rev, N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fp * fw, N)
    M = 2 * n - 2
    idx = M - np.arange(n)
    H = conv[idx]
    H = np.where(np.isfinite(H), H, 0.0)
    return np.maximum(H, 0.0)


def _z_fun(z, D, block, n, tau):
    p = _z_to_p(z, D, block, n)
    b = fft_square(p, n)
    if b.size == 0:
        return 1e100
    s = 2.0 * n * b
    s = 0.5 * (s + s[::-1])
    peak = float(np.max(s))
    if not np.isfinite(peak):
        return 1e100
    tau = float(max(1e-6, tau))
    ez = np.exp((s - peak) / tau)
    ez = np.where(np.isfinite(ez), ez, 0.0)
    wsum = float(np.sum(ez))
    if not np.isfinite(wsum) or wsum <= 0.0:
        return peak
    return float(peak + tau * np.log(wsum))


def _z_grad(z, D, block, n, tau):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    D = int(D)
    block = int(block)
    n = int(n)
    if z.size != D or D * block != n:
        return np.zeros_like(z, dtype=np.float64)

    q = _softmax_z(z)
    p = _q_to_p(q, block, n)
    b = fft_square(p, n)
    if b.size == 0:
        return np.zeros_like(z, dtype=np.float64)

    s = 2.0 * n * b
    s = 0.5 * (s + s[::-1])
    peak = float(np.max(s))
    if not np.isfinite(peak):
        return np.zeros_like(z, dtype=np.float64)

    tau = float(max(1e-6, tau))
    ez = np.exp((s - peak) / tau)
    ez = np.where(np.isfinite(ez), ez, 0.0)
    wsum = float(np.sum(ez))
    if (not np.isfinite(wsum)) or wsum <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(s.shape, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(s))] = 1.0
        wsum = 1.0
    else:
        w = ez / wsum

    H = weighted_shift_sum_fft(p, w)
    g = 4.0 * n * H
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)

    Gmean = g.reshape(D, block).sum(axis=1) / float(block)
    S = float(np.dot(p, g))
    grad = q * (Gmean - S)
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return grad


def active_gradient(p, b, tau, max_active, select_delta):
    """
    Subgradient for a selected set of near-maximum convolution coefficients.
    Returns (smooth_approx_value, gradient_array).
    """
    n = len(p)
    s = 2.0 * n * b
    s = 0.5 * (s + s[::-1])
    if s.size == 0:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    peak = float(np.max(s))
    if not np.isfinite(peak):
        return 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    tau = float(max(1e-6, tau))
    delta = float(max(select_delta, 3.0 * tau, 1e-9))

    mask = s >= peak - delta
    if not np.any(mask):
        active = np.array([int(np.argmax(s))], dtype=int)
    else:
        active = np.where(mask)[0]
        max_active = int(max(1, max_active))
        if active.size > max_active:
            active = np.argpartition(s, -max_active)[-max_active:]

    ez = np.exp((s[active] - peak) / tau)
    ez = np.where(np.isfinite(ez), ez, 0.0)
    wsum = float(np.sum(ez))
    if (not np.isfinite(wsum)) or wsum <= 0.0:
        active = np.array([int(np.argmax(s))], dtype=int)
        wsum = 1.0
        weights = np.ones(active.shape, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        weights = ez / wsum

    H = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k, wgt in zip(active, weights):
        if wgt <= 0.0:
            continue
        k = int(k)
        if k < n:
            H[:k + 1] += wgt * p[k::-1]
        else:
            t = k - n + 1
            H[t:] += wgt * p[n - 1:t - 1:-1]

    g = 4.0 * n * H
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    val = float(peak + tau * np.log(max(wsum, 1e-300)))
    return val, g


def mirror_optimize(p, n, max_seconds, tau, max_active, select_delta):
    """
    Multiplicative active-peak descent with a simple line search.
    """
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(max_seconds))
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if p.size != n:
        p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        p /= s

    best = p.copy()
    best_score = fft_score_array(p)
    eta = 0.25
    stall = 0
    iter_count = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        b = fft_square(p, n)
        if b.size == 0:
            break
        sc = 2.0 * n * b
        peak = float(np.max(sc))
        if not np.isfinite(peak):
            break
        _, g = active_gradient(p, b, tau, max_active, select_delta)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            break
        maxg = float(np.max(g))
        if maxg <= 1e-14:
            break

        accepted = False
        for trial in (eta, 0.5 * eta, 0.25 * eta):
            if trial <= 1e-5:
                break
            center = float(np.mean(g))
            exp_arg = -trial * (g - center)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(exp_arg)):
                break
            np.clip(exp_arg, -40.0, 40.0, out=exp_arg)
            fac = np.exp(exp_arg)
            pn = p * fac
            pn = np.where(np.isfinite(pn), pn, 0.0)
            pn = np.maximum(pn, 0.0)
            sn = float(np.sum(pn))
            if sn <= 0.0:
                continue
            pn /= sn
            new_score = fft_score_array(pn)
            if np.isfinite(new_score) and new_score < peak - 1e-12:
                p = pn
                peak = new_score
                if new_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best = p.copy()
                    best_score = new_score
                eta = min(1.0, eta * 1.12)
                stall = 0
                accepted = True
                break

        if not accepted:
            stall += 1
            eta *= 0.65
            if eta < 1e-4 and stall > 30:
                break

        iter_count += 1

    if fft_score_array(best) > best_score:
        best = p.copy()
    return best


def resize_sequence_to_length(a, m):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(max(1, int(m)), dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    m = int(max(1, m))
    if a.size == m:
        return a.copy()
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    y = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    out = np.interp(y, x, a)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def geometric_fallback(n, alpha=1.0):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    denom = max(1, n - 1)
    p = np.exp(-float(alpha) * x / float(denom))
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        p /= s
    return p


def make_z_starts(D, rng, extra_seq=None):
    D = int(max(2, D))
    x = (np.arange(D, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(D)
    starts = []

    def add(z):
        z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if z.size != D:
            return
        z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
        if np.max(np.abs(z)) < 0.25:
            z += 0.02 * rng.normal(size=D)
        z -= float(np.mean(z))
        starts.append(z.copy())

    # Linear / geometric-like profiles.
    for amp in (0.8, -0.8, 1.4, -1.4):
        add(amp * (x - 0.5))

    # Low-frequency waves.
    for phase in (0.0, 1.0, 2.3):
        add(0.9 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x + phase))
    add(0.7 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x) + 0.4 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x))
    add(0.6 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x) - 0.35 * np.sin(6.0 * np.pi * x))

    # Random smooth profiles.
    for _ in range(3):
        z = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
        for f in range(1, 4):
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 1.0))
             ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            z += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph)
        add(0.8 * z)

    # Gaussian peak profiles.
    for c, wdt in ((0.18, 0.09), (0.82, 0.09), (0.5, 0.16)):
        pshape = 1.0 + 3.0 * np.exp(-((x - c) / wdt) ** 2)
        add(np.log(np.maximum(pshape, 1e-6)))

    # Optional imported good-ish sequence.
    if extra_seq is not None:
        try:
            h = resize_sequence_to_length(extra_seq, D)
            h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
            h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
            hs = float(np.sum(h))
            if hs > 0.0:
                h = h / hs
                z = np.log(np.maximum(h, 1e-8))
                add(z)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if not starts:
        starts.append(np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64))
    return starts


def optimize_piecewise(n, D, block, z_starts, deadline, best_p, best_score,
                       max_starts=6, stage_defs=None):
    if not _HAS_SCIPY:
        return best_p, best_score

    if stage_defs is None:
        stage_defs = (
            (0.15, 60),
            (0.08, 100),
            (0.04, 120),
            (0.02, 160),
        )

    n = int(n)
    D = int(D)
    block = int(block)

    for z0 in z_starts[:max_starts]:
        if time.monotonic() + 120.0 > deadline:
            break
        z = np.asarray(z0, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
        if z.size != D:
            continue
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
            continue

        for tau, maxiter in stage_defs:
            if time.monotonic() + 60.0 > deadline:
                break
            maxiter = int(max(20, maxiter))
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    _z_fun,
                    z,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    jac=_z_grad,
                    args=(D, block, n, float(tau)),
                    options={
                        "maxiter": maxiter,
                        "maxfun": 3 * maxiter,
                        "maxls": 20,
                        "ftol": 1e-7,
                        "gtol": 1e-5,
                        "maxcor": 10,
                    },
                )
                if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x"):
                    nz = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                    if nz.size == D and np.all(np.isfinite(nz)):
                        z = nz
            except Exception:
                pass

            z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if z.size != D or not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
                continue
            z -= float(np.mean(z))

            p = _z_to_p(z, D, block, n)
            ff = fft_score_array(p)

            # Exact score when there is a little time to spare.
            if time.monotonic() + 0.25 < deadline:
                ex = direct_score_array(p)
                if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < best_score:
                    best_score = ex
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    print(f"piecewise n={n} D={D} score={ex:.7f} fft={ff:.7f}")

    return best_p, best_score


def zeroize_candidate(p, factor=1e-7):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if p.size == 0:
        return p
    mx = float(np.max(p))
    if mx <= 0.0:
        return p
    p[p < mx * float(factor)] = 0.0
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0:
        return p
    return p / s


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """Return a nonnegative sequence with a low exact evaluation score."""
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1800.0
    budget = max(60.0, budget)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(5.0, budget - 5.0)

    if seed is None:
        seed = 20240501
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Choose resolution based on available time.
    if budget < 300.0:
        n, D = 1024, 128
    elif budget < 900.0:
        n, D = 2048, 256
    else:
        n, D = 4096, 512
    block = max(1, n // D)
    D = n // block
    if D * block != n:
        D = max(1, n // max(1, block))
        block = n // D
        n = D * block

    # Initial best: a smooth one-sided profile already better than uniform.
    best_p = geometric_fallback(n, 1.2)
    best_score = direct_score_array(best_p)

    # Directly compare with any imported previous sequence, if present.
    try:
        hseq = resize_sequence_to_length(height_sequence_1, n)  # noqa: F821
        hseq = np.where(np.isfinite(hseq), hseq, 0.0)
        hseq = np.maximum(hseq, 0.0)
        hs = float(np.sum(hseq))
        if hs > 0:
            hseq /= hs
            hscore = direct_score_array(hseq)
            if np.isfinite(hscore) and hscore < best_score:
                best_score = hscore
                best_p = hseq.copy()
    except Exception:
        pass

    extra = None
    try:
        extra = height_sequence_1  # noqa: F821
    except Exception:
        extra = None

    z_starts = make_z_starts(D, rng, extra)

    # Primary piecewise optimization.
    stage_deadline = deadline - 700.0
    if _HAS_SCIPY and time.monotonic() < stage_deadline:
        max_starts = max(2, min(8, len(z_starts)))
        best_p, best_score = optimize_piecewise(
            n, D, block, z_starts, stage_deadline, best_p, best_score,
            max_starts=max_starts
        )

        # If strong remaining time and still not especially low, run a little
        # extra piecewise from the same best z is implicit; otherwise continue.
    elif not _HAS_SCIPY:
        # Fallback without scipy: mirror descent from several smooth starts.
        rem = max(0.0, deadline - 60.0 - time.monotonic())
        for z0 in z_starts[:min(6, len(z_starts))]:
            if time.monotonic() + 10.0 > deadline:
                break
            sec = max(10.0, rem * 0.15)
            p = _z_to_p(z0, D, block, n)
            p = mirror_optimize(p, n, sec, 0.1, 64, 0.05)
            ex = direct_score_array(p)
            if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < best_score:
                best_score = ex
                best_p = p.copy()
                print(f"no-sym-mirror score={ex:.7f}")

    # Full-resolution softmax polish with L-BFGS, if available.
    if _HAS_SCIPY and time.monotonic() < deadline - 220.0:
        zfull = np.log(np.maximum(best_p, 1e-300))
        zfull = np.where(np.isfinite(zfull), zfull, 0.0)
        zfull -= float(np.mean(zfull))
        full_stages = (
            (0.08, 90),
            (0.04, 120),
            (0.02, 140),
        )
        for tau, maxiter in full_stages:
            if time.monotonic() + 80.0 > deadline:
                break
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    _z_fun,
                    zfull,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    jac=_z_grad,
                    args=(n, 1, n, float(tau)),
                    options={
                        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                        "maxfun": 3 * int(maxiter),
                        "maxls": 20,
                        "ftol": 1e-7,
                        "gtol": 1e-5,
                        "maxcor": 10,
                    },
                )
                if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x"):
                    nf = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                    if nf.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(nf)):
                        zfull = nf
            except Exception:
                pass

            pf = _z_to_p(zfull, n, 1, n)
            if time.monotonic() + 0.25 < deadline:
                ex = direct_score_array(pf)
                if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < best_score:
                    best_score = ex
                    best_p = pf.copy()
                    print(f"full-lbfgs score={ex:.7f}")

    # Final multipactive polishing in several temperature regimes.
    rem_mirror = max(0.0, (deadline - 40.0) - time.monotonic())
    if rem_mirror > 20.0:
        plan = (
            (0.10, 0.50),
            (0.05, 0.35),
            (0.02, 0.15),
        )
        for tau, frac in plan:
            sec = rem_mirror * frac
            if sec <= 5.0 or time.monotonic() + 5.0 > deadline:
                continue
            best_p = mirror_optimize(best_p, n, sec, float(tau), 64, 0.05)
            if time.monotonic() + 0.25 < deadline:
                ex = direct_score_array(best_p)
                if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < best_score:
                    best_score = ex
                    best_p = best_p.copy()
                    print(f"mirror score={ex:.7f}")

    # Try sparsifying tiny values, which can sometimes help exact score.
    base_score = direct_score_array(best_p) if np.isfinite(best_score) else best_score
    for factor in (1e-8, 1e-7, 1e-6, 5e-6):
        p2 = zeroize_candidate(best_p, factor)
        if p2.size != n:
            continue
        sc2 = direct_score_array(p2)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = sc2
            best_p = p2.copy()
            print(f"zeroize factor={factor:.0e} score={best_score:.7f}")

    # Final sanitization and validation.
    best_p = np.asarray(best_p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_p = np.where(np.isfinite(best_p), best_p, 0.0)
    best_p = np.clip(best_p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    ss = float(np.sum(best_p))
    if ss <= 0.0:
        best_p = geometric_fallback(n, 1.2)
        best_score = direct_score_array(best_p)
    else:
        best_p /= ss

    if base_score < best_score:
        best_score = base_score

    out = [float(v) for v in best_p]

    try:
        exact = evaluate_sequence(out)  # noqa: F821
        if np.isfinite(exact):
            print(f"final validated score={exact:.7f} length={len(out)}")
        else:
            print("final validation inf, falling back")
            best_p = geometric_fallback(n, 1.2)
            out = [float(v) for v in best_p]
    except NameError:
        pass
    except Exception:
        pass

    return out
```
